To SAV Entry Page     
  
          ABOUT SAV... 
          The Ship Availability Database (SAV) contains historical 
information on US Naval vessels and completed shipyard availabilities. 
Shipyard availability is defined as a period of time that a ship is scheduled to undergo construction, critical repairs or maintenance, modernization, conversion, or other work that typically can only be accomplished in a shipyard. Shipyard availabilities can last from a few days to several years depending on the size of the availability work package.  
          The SAV database contains approximately 15,000 completed 
availabilites from 1913 to 2001. The SAV database does not contain all availabilities for all US Naval vessels during this time period. Every attempt has been made to ensure that the data presented is correct, however, it is possible that some minor inaccuracy may exist. Please report any information you may have via the SAV “Feedback Button”. It is important that you also provide your source of data that is in conflict with the SAV data presented here. Annual updates are planned for the database.  
  
This information resides on a DoD interest computer. Important conditions, restrictions, and disclaimers apply
